{ValidationResult} from './validation.js';\nimport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport {\n  createUserEntry,\n  createAssistantEntry,\n  createThinkingEntry,\n  createToolEntry,\n} from '../../orchestration/entryFactories.js';\nimport {appendEntry, appendDelta, commitEntry, markTruncated} from '../../orchestration/entryMutations.js';\nimport {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd} from './sender.js';\nimport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n\ninterface StreamState {\n  thinkingIndex: number | null;\n  assistantIndex: number | null;\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n  sentAssistantIndices: number[];\n}\n\ntype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\ntype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\nexport function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, validation));\n  };\n}\n\n// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\nasync function answerInTurn(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n    return;\n  }\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  try {\n    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n  } finally {\n    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n  }\n}\n\n// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\nfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n  if (signal?.aborted) {\n    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n}\n\nasync function answerUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n}\n\nfunction answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, validation: ValidationResult): void {\n  if (!validation.valid) {\n    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const message = validation.data;\n  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n}\n\n// Read from the raw frame, so a user_message that fails validation still gets its stream_end.\nfunction isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage: unknown): boolean {\n  return typeof rawMessage === 'object' && rawMessage !== null && Reflect.get(rawMessage, 'type') === 'user_message';\n}\n\nasync function wsErrorHandler(ws: WebSocket, action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await action();\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';\n    const code = error instanceof AIError ? error.code : 'UNKNOWN';\n    log.error('message', 'handling error', {error: message, code});\n    if (error instanceof AIError && error.code === 'RATE_LIMITED') {\n      sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.RATE_LIMITED, 'AI service is busy, please try again');\n      return;\n    }\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Failed to process message');\n  }\n}\n\nasync function handleUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  content: string,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  log.info('message', 'user message received', {sessionId: session.sessionId, contentLength: content.length});\n\n  const userEntry = createUserEntry(content);\n  const userIndex = appendEntry(session, userEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, userIndex, userEntry);\n\n  const parser = new ThinkingTagParser();\n  const state: StreamState = {\n    thinkingIndex: null,\n    assistantIndex: null,\n    suppressOutput: false,\n    sentAssistantIndices: [],\n  };\n\n  let textChunkCount = 0;\n\n  try {\n    for await (const chunk of orchestrator.processMessageStream(session, {signal})) {\n      // Its socket closed: breaking returns the run's generator, so the run stops and its own teardown still runs.\n      if (signal?.aborted) break;\n      textChunkCount = logChunk(chunk, textChunkCount);\n      processChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk);\n    }\n  } finally {\n    // Anything still open here was cut short: a stop has already flushed the parser and committed every entry it opened.\n    flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n  }\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n  log.info('stream', 'stream completed', {suppressed: state.suppressOutput, entries: session.entries.length});\n}\n\n// Diagnostics only: counts a run of text chunks so it is logged once, not per token.\nfunction logChunk(chunk: MessageChunk, textChunkCount: number): number {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return textChunkCount + 1;\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') {\n    log.debug('stream', 'tool_result chunk', {\n      messageCount: chunk.clientMessages.length,\n      types: chunk.clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n    });\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  log.debug('stream', 'chunk', {type: chunk.type});\n  return 0;\n}\n\nfunction processChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  chunk: MessageChunk\n): void {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return processTextChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk.text);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_use') return processToolUseChunk(ws, session, chunk.toolCall);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') return processToolResultChunk(ws, chunk.clientMessages);\n  if (chunk.type === 'suppress_output') return processSuppressChunk(ws, session, state);\n  if (chunk.type === 'stop') return processStopChunk(ws, session, state, parser);\n}\n\nfunction processTextChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  text: string\n): void {\n  const segments = parser.push(text);\n  for (const segment of segments) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction handleTextSegment(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, segment: ParsedSegment): void {\n  if (segment.type === 'thinking') {\n    streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex', createThinkingEntry, segment.content);\n    return;\n  }\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex', createAssistantEntry, segment.content);\n}\n\nfunction streamSegment(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  key: StateIndexKey,\n  createEntry: EntryFactory,\n  content: string\n): void {\n  const isNew = initSegment(session, state, key, createEntry);\n  if (isNew && state.suppressOutput) skipSuppressed(ws, session, state[key]!);\n  if (isNew && !state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, state[key]!, session.entries[state[key]!]);\n  appendDelta(session, state[key]!, content);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendDelta(ws, state[key]!, content);\n}\n\n// Stored as suppressed, so a replay on resume shows the client what it saw live and not the text a tool hid.\nfunction skipSuppressed(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  markSuppressed(session, index);\n  sendSkip(ws, index);\n}\n\nfunction markSuppressed(session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  const entry = session.entries[index];\n  if (entry.role === 'assistant' || entry.role === 'thinking') entry.suppressed = true;\n}\n\nfunction initSegment(session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey, createEntry: EntryFactory): boolean {\n  if (state[key] !== null) return false;\n  state[key] = appendEntry(session, createEntry());\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction commitAndClear(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n\n  commitEntry(session, index);\n  state[key] = null;\n\n  // Only what the client received can be retracted, and only assistant entries.\n  if (state.suppressOutput) return;\n\n  sendCommit(ws, index);\n  if (key === 'assistantIndex') state.sentAssistantIndices.push(index);\n}\n\nfunction processToolUseChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  toolCall: {name: string; input: Record<string, unknown>}\n): void {\n  const toolEntry = createToolEntry(toolCall.name, toolCall.input);\n  const toolIndex = appendEntry(session, toolEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, toolIndex, toolEntry);\n}\n\nfunction processToolResultChunk(ws: WebSocket, clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[]): void {\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    log.debug('stream', 'forwarding client message to frontend', {\n      type: msg.type,\n      index: 'index' in msg ? msg.index : undefined,\n    });\n    sendJson(ws, msg);\n  }\n}\n\n// Retracts what the client saw: every assistant entry sent so far is re-sent blank.\nfunction processSuppressChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  // Closed as streamed so far: an open answer is then retracted like a sent one, and what follows opens new entries.\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n  log.debug('stream', 'suppression activated', {blankingEntries: state.sentAssistantIndices});\n\n  for (const idx of state.sentAssistantIndices) {\n    sendUpsert(ws, idx, {...session.entries[idx], content: ''} as SessionEntry);\n    markSuppressed(session, idx);\n  }\n\n  state.sentAssistantIndices = [];\n  state.suppressOutput = true;\n}\n\nfunction processStopChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n}\n\n// The parser holds back a tail that might open a tag; without this the tail of a cut-off answer would be lost.\nfunction flushHeldText(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  for (const segment of parser.flush()) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\n// The flag lives on the stored entry even when suppressed; only a visible entry is re-sent, which is how a live client learns it.\nfunction closeCutOff(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n  markTruncated(session, index);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, index, session.entries[index]);\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, key);\n}\n\nfunction commitOpenEntries(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n}\n\nfunction handlePing(ws: WebSocket, timestamp: number): void {\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'pong', timestamp}));\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":8,"oldLines":6,"newStart":8,"newLines":7,"lines":[" import type const isNew = initSegment(session, state, key, createEntry);\n  if (isNew && state.suppressOutput) skipSuppressed(ws, session, state[key]!);\n  if (isNew && !state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, StateIndexKey, createEntry: EntryFactory): boolean {\n  if (state[key] !== null) return false;\n  state[key] = appendEntry(session, createEntry());\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction commitAndClear(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n\n  commitEntry(session, index);\n  state[key] = null;\n\n  // Only what the client received can be retracted, and only assistant entries.\n  if (state.suppressOutput) return;\n\n  sendCommit(ws, index);\n  if (key === 'assistantIndex') state.sentAssistantIndices.push(index);\n}\n\nfunction processToolUseChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  toolCall: {name: string; input: Record<string, unknown>}\n): void {\n  const toolEntry = createToolEntry(toolCall.name, toolCall.input);\n  const toolIndex = appendEntry(session, toolEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, toolIndex, toolEntry);\n}\n\nfunction processToolResultChunk(ws: WebSocket, clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[]): void {\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    log.debug('stream', 'forwarding client message to frontend', {\n      type: msg.type,\n      index: 'index' in msg ? msg.index : undefined,\n    });\n    sendJson(ws, msg);\n  }\n}\n\n// Retracts what the client saw: every assistant entry sent so far is re-sent blank.\nfunction processSuppressChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  // Closed as streamed so far: an open answer is then retracted like a sent one, and what follows opens new entries.\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n  log.debug('stream', 'suppression activated', {blankingEntries: state.sentAssistantIndices});\n\n  for (const idx of state.sentAssistantIndices) {\n    sendUpsert(ws, idx, {...session.entries[idx], content: ''} as SessionEntry);\n    markSuppressed(session, idx);\n  }\n\n  state.sentAssistantIndices = [];\n  state.suppressOutput = true;\n}\n\nfunction processStopChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n}\n\n// The parser holds back a tail that might open a tag; without this the tail of a cut-off answer would be lost.\nfunction flushHeldText(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  for (const segment of parser.flush()) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\n// The flag lives on the stored entry {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n  markTruncated(session, index);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, index, session.entries[index]);\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, key);\n}\n\nfunction commitOpenEntries(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  commitAndClear(ws, [];","originalFile":"import http from 'http';\nimport {WebSocketServer} from 'ws';\nimport type {Express} from 'express';\nimport type {WsAuthenticator} from '../types/auth.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {ToolRegistry} from '../orchestration/tools/registry.js';\nimport {createUpgradeHandler, createConnectionHandler, ExtWebSocket} from './ws/connectionHandler.js';\nimport {createMessageHandler} from './ws/messageHandler.js';\nimport {selectSubprotocol} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\nimport type {ConnectInfo, SessionResumeOptions} from './ws/sessionResume.js';\nimport {log} from '../shared/logger.js';\n\nexport interface HalServerOptions {\n  app: Express;\n  wsAuth: WsAuthenticator;\n  sessionStore: SessionStore;\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator;\n  toolRegistry?: ToolRegistry;\n  basePath?: string;\n  port?: number;\n  heartbeatIntervalMs?: number;\n  onConnect?: (session: import('../types/session.js').ChatSession, connection: ConnectInfo) => void | Promise<void>;\n  onDisconnect?: (sessionId: string) => void | Promise<void>;\n  resume?: SessionResumeOptions;\n}\n\nexport interface HalServer {\n  server: http.Server;\n  wss: WebSocketServer;\n  start: (port?: number) => Promise<void>;\n  stop: () => Promise<void>;\n}\n\nexport function createServer(options: HalServerOptions): HalServer {\n  const basePath = options.basePath ?? '/hal';\n\n  const wss = new WebSocketServer({\n    noServer: true,\n    handleProtocols: selectSubprotocol,\n  });\n\n  const handleMessage = createMessageHandler(options.orchestrator);\n  const examplePrompts = options.toolRegistry?.getExamplePrompts() ?? [];\n\n  const deps = {\n    wsAuth: options.wsAuth,\n    sessionStore: options.sessionStore,\n    handleMessage,\n    basePath,\n    onConnect: options.onConnect,\n    onDisconnect: options.onDisconnect,\n    resume: options.resume,\n  };\n\n  const handleUpgrade = createUpgradeHandler(wss, deps);\n  const handleConnection = createConnectionHandler(deps, examplePrompts);\n\n  const server = http.createServer(options.app);\n\n  server.on('upgrade', (request, socket, head) => {\n    handleUpgrade(request, socket, head);\n  });\n\n  wss.on('connection', ws => {\n    handleConnection(ws as ExtWebSocket);\n  });\n\n  const heartbeatMs = options.heartbeatIntervalMs ?? 30_000;\n\n  const heartbeatInterval = setInterval(() => {\n    wss.clients.forEach(ws => {\n      const extWs = ws as ExtWebSocket;\n      if (!extWs.isAlive) {\n        extWs.terminate();\n        return;\n      }\n      extWs.isAlive = false;\n      extWs.ping();\n    });\n  }, heartbeatMs);\n\n  // The listening socket should hold the process open, not the heartbeat: an engine never started must not.\n  heartbeatInterval.unref();\n\n  wss.on('close', () => clearInterval(heartbeatInterval));\n\n  return {\n    server,\n    wss,\n    start: (port?: number) =>\n      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n        // Refused before the handlers are touched: `listen` throws on a running server, and the swap below stripped its handler.\n        if (server.listening) {\n          reject(new Error('HAL Engine is already started'));\n          return;\n        }\n\n        // ?? not ||, so a configured port 0 means \"let the OS choose\" rather than 8086.\n        const p = port ?? options.port ?? (Number(process.env.PORT) || 8086);\n\n        // Without this an EADDRINUSE settles nothing and takes the process down as an unhandled event.\n        const onError = (error: Error) => reject(error);\n        server.once('error', onError);\n\n        // A listener left from a previous start would report this bind failure as a running server breaking.\n        server.removeListener('error', logLateError);\n\n        server.listen(p, () => {\n          server.removeListener('error', onError);\n\n          // Past the listen window nothing is listening, and an 'error' with no handler ends the process.\n          server.on('error', logLateError);\n\n          // The bound port, not the requested one: a configured 0 means the OS chose it.\n          log.info('server', 'HAL Engine started', {port: boundPort(server, p)});\n          resolve();\n        });\n      }),\n    stop: () =>\n      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n        wss.clients.forEach(ws => ws.close(1001, 'Server shutting down'));\n        wss.close();\n        clearInterval(heartbeatInterval);\n\n        // Nothing to close after a refused start or a second stop: `close` would reject for a server that never ran.\n        if (!server.listening) {\n          resolve();\n          return;\n        }\n        server.close(err => (err ? reject(err) : resolve()));\n      }),\n  };\n}\n\n// A running server's error is a condition to report, not a reason to exit; the consumer decides what to do.\nfunction logLateError(error: Error): void {\n  log.error('server', 'server error after start', {error: error.message});\n}\n\nfunction boundPort(server: http.Server, requested: number): number {\n  const address = server.address();\n  return typeof address === 'object' && address !== null ? address.port : requested;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":40,"oldLines":7,"newStart":40,"newLines":7,"lines":["     handleProtocols: selectSubprotocol,","   });"," ","-  const handleMessage = createMessageHandler(options.orchestrator);","+  const handleMessage = createMessageHandler(options.orchestrator, options.sessionStore);","   const examplePrompts = options.toolRegistry?.getExamplePrompts() ?? [];"," ","   const deps = {"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01K16VjA8gkqzasZRrP4qZvo","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

'ws';\n3\timport {createMessageHandler} from './messageHandler.js';\n4\timport {createChatOrchestrator, TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\n5\timport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\n6\timport {ToolRegistry} from '../../orchestration/tools/registry.js';\n7\timport {PromptBuilder} from '../../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\n8\timport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk, ToolCall} from '../../types/ai.js';\n9\timport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\n10\timport type {ChatSession, SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\n11\timport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\n12\timport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\n13\timport {deferred} from '../wsTestSupport.js';\n14\t\n15\t// The frames, in order, are the contract - above all for suppression, which retracts.\n16\t\n17\t// One line per frame; entries carry generated timestamps no assertion should read.\n18\tconst wire = (sent: OutgoingMessage[]): string[] =>\n19\t  sent.map(frame => {\n20\t    if (frame.type === 'entry_upsert') {\n21\t      const content = 'content' in frame.entry ? frame.entry.content : '';\n22\t      return `upsert ${frame.index} ${frame.entry.role} ${JSON.stringify(content)}`;\n23\t    }\n24\t    if (frame.type === 'entry_delta') return `delta ${frame.index} ${JSON.stringify(frame.delta)}`;\n25\t    if (frame.type === 'entry_commit') return `commit ${frame.index}`;\n26\t    if (frame.type === 'entry_skip') return `skip ${frame.index}`;\n27\t    if (frame.type === 'error') return `error ${frame.code}`;\n28\t    if (frame.type === 'pong') return `pong ${frame.timestamp}`;\n29\t    return frame.type;\n30\t  });\n31\t\n32\tconst text = (value: string): MessageChunk => ({type: 'text', text: value});\n33\tconst STOP: MessageChunk = {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n34\tconst SUPPRESS = {type: 'suppress_output'} as unknown as MessageChunk;\n35\t\n36\tinterface StreamScript {\n37\t  failWith?: Error;\n38\t  pauseAfter?: {chunks: number; until: Promise<void>};\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\tconst harness = (chunks: MessageChunk[], {failWith, pauseAfter}: StreamScript = {}) => {\n42\t  const sent: OutgoingMessage[] = [];\n43\t  const ws = {send: (raw: string) => sent.push(JSON.parse(raw) as OutgoingMessage)} as unknown as WebSocket;\n44\t\n45\t  const stream = async function* (): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n46\t    for (const [position, chunk] of chunks.entries()) {\n47\t      if (pauseAfter && position === pauseAfter.chunks) await pauseAfter.until;\n48\t      yield chunk;\n49\t    }\n50\t    if (failWith) throw failWith;\n51\t  };\n52\t\n53\t  const processMessageStream = jest.fn(stream);\n54\t  const orchestrator = {processMessageStream} as unknown as ChatOrchestrator;\n55\t  const session: ChatSession = {sessionId: 's1', userId: 'u1', entries: []};\n56\t\n57\t  const handle = createMessageHandler(orchestrator);\n58\t\n59\t  return {\n60\t    session,\n61\t    processMessageStream,\n62\t    sent,\n63\t    frames: () => wire(sent),\n64\t    send: (raw: unknown = {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}, signal?: AbortSignal) =>\n65\t      handle(ws, session, raw, signal),\n66\t  };\n67\t};\n68\t\n69\tdescribe('the websocket message handler', () => {\n70\t  describe('dispatch', () => {\n71\t    it('rejects an unparseable message and never starts a stream', async () => {\n72\t      const h = harness([]);\n73\t\n74\t      await h.send({type: 'nonsense'});\n75\t\n76\t      const {calls} = h.processMessageStream.mock;\n77\t\n78\t      expect({frames: h.frames(), streams: calls.length}).toEqual({frames: ['error INVALID_MESSAGE'], streams: 0});\n79\t    });\n80\t\n81\t    it('answers a ping with the timestamp it was given', async () => {\n82\t      const h = harness([]);\n83\t\n84\t      await h.send({type: 'ping', timestamp: 1234});\n85\t\n86\t      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['pong 1234']);\n87\t    });\n88\t  });\n89\t\n90\t  describe('a plain assistant reply', () => {\n91\t    it('sends the user entry, then the assistant entry, its delta, its commit and stream_end', async () => {\n92\t      const h = harness([text('hi there'), STOP]);\n93\t\n94\t      await h.send();\n95\t\n96\t      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n97\t        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n98\t        'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n99\t        'delta 1 \"hi there\"',\n100\t        'commit 1',"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd02a1d7-8f6a-4e12-a9ac-a9bf647f6c20","uuid":"efc6b9cb-caad-4ae4-8cd0-c65e3c339fe5","timestamp":"2026-11-03T12:39:40.784Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/d86c758d8bbe/workspace/target/src/transport/ws/messageHandler.test.ts","content":"import {deferred} from '../wsTestSupport.js';\n\n// The frames, in order, are the contract - above all for suppression, which retracts.\n\n// One line per frame; entries carry generated timestamps no assertion should read.\nconst wire = (sent: OutgoingMessage[]): string[] =>\n  sent.map(frame => {\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_upsert') {\n      const content = 'content' in frame.entry ? frame.entry.content : '';\n      return `upsert ${frame.index} ${frame.entry.role} ${JSON.stringify(content)}`;\n    }\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_delta') return `delta ${frame.index} ${JSON.stringify(frame.delta)}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_commit') return `commit ${frame.index}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_skip') return `skip ${frame.index}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'error') return `error ${frame.code}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'pong') return `pong ${frame.timestamp}`;\n    return frame.type;\n  });\n\nconst text = (value: string): MessageChunk => ({type: 'text', text: value});\nconst STOP: MessageChunk = {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\nconst SUPPRESS = {type: 'suppress_output'} as unknown as MessageChunk;\n\ninterface StreamScript {\n  failWith?: Error;\n  pauseAfter?: {chunks: number; until: Promise<void>};\n}\n\nconst harness = (chunks: MessageChunk[], {failWith, pauseAfter}: StreamScript = {}) => {\n  const sent: OutgoingMessage[] = [];\n  const ws = {send: (raw: string) => sent.push(JSON.parse(raw) as OutgoingMessage)} as unknown as WebSocket;\n\n  const stream = async function* (): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    for (const [position, chunk] of chunks.entries()) {\n      if (pauseAfter && position === pauseAfter.chunks) await pauseAfter.until;\n      yield chunk;\n    }\n    if (failWith) throw failWith;\n  };\n\n  const processMessageStream = jest.fn(stream);\n  const orchestrator = {processMessageStream} as unknown as ChatOrchestrator;\n  const session: ChatSession = {sessionId: 's1', userId: 'u1', entries: []};\n\n  const handle = createMessageHandler(orchestrator);\n\n  return {\n    session,\n    processMessageStream,\n    sent,\n    frames: () => wire(sent),\n    send: (raw: unknown = {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}, signal?: AbortSignal) =>\n      handle(ws, session, raw, signal),\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('the websocket message handler', () => {\n  describe('dispatch', () => {\n    it('rejects an unparseable message and never starts a stream', async () => {\n      const h = harness([]);\n\n      await h.send({type: 'nonsense'});\n\n      const {calls} = h.processMessageStream.mock;\n\n      expect({frames: h.frames(), streams: calls.length}).toEqual({frames: ['error INVALID_MESSAGE'], streams: 0});\n    });\n\n    it('answers a ping with the timestamp it was given', async () => {\n      const h = harness([]);\n\n      await h.send({type: 'ping', timestamp: 1234});\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['pong 1234']);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('a plain assistant reply', () => {\n    it('sends the user entry, then the assistant entry, its delta, its commit and stream_end', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('hi there'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n        'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n        'delta 1 \"hi there\"',\n        'commit 1',","numLines":100,"startLine":1,"totalLines":870}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01HAqfAQbXCAkubCFF7oNXac","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_011Cgg1WkWSSmvy9uB235soF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WY1rEQ2ZSoo3c3F4AWBmKT","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/d86c758d8bbe/workspace/target/src/transport/ws/messageHandler.ts","old_string":"export function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator, sessionStore?: SessionStore) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, session, sessionStore, validation));\n  };\n}","new_string":"export function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator, sessionStore?: SessionStore) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, session, sessionStore, validation));\n  };\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":8,"cache_creation_input_tokens":689,"cache_read_input_tokens":64057,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":689},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd02a1d7-8f6a-4e12-a9ac-a9bf647f6c20","uuid":"5e18f2bb-f0e8-409f-807e-b64f4b3a0006","timestamp":"2026-11-03T12:40:00.862Z","request_id":"req_011Cgg1WkGZ1eQTdpBkVhRRv"}

'../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../../types/sessionStore.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../../orchestration/entryFactories.js';\nimport {appendEntry, appendDelta, commitEntry, markTruncated} from '../../orchestration/entryMutations.js';\nimport {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd, sendSessionList} from './sender.js';\nimport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n\ninterface StreamState {\n  thinkingIndex: number | null;\n  assistantIndex: number | null;\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n  sentAssistantIndices: number[];\n}\n\ntype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\ntype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\nexport function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator, sessionStore?: SessionStore) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, session, sessionStore, validation));\n  };\n}\n\n// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\nasync function answerInTurn(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n    return;\n  }\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  try {\n    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n  } finally {\n    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n  }\n}\n\n// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\nfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n  if (signal?.aborted) {\n    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n}\n\nasync function answerUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n}\n\nfunction answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, sessionStore: SessionStore | undefined, validation: ValidationResult): void {\n  if (!validation.valid) {\n    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const message = validation.data;\n  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n  if (message.type === 'list_sessions') handleListSessions(ws, session, sessionStore, message);\n}\n\n// Read from the raw frame, so a user_message that fails validation still gets its stream_end.\nfunction isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage: unknown): boolean {\n  return typeof rawMessage === 'object' && rawMessage !== null && Reflect.get(rawMessage, 'type') === 'user_message';\n}\n\nasync function wsErrorHandler(ws: WebSocket, action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await action();\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';\n    const code = error instanceof AIError ? error.code : 'UNKNOWN';\n    log.error('message', 'handling error', {error: message, code});\n    if (error instanceof AIError && error.code === 'RATE_LIMITED') {\n      sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.RATE_LIMITED, 'AI service is busy, please try again');\n      return;\n    }\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Failed to process message');\n  }\n}\n\nasync function handleUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  content: string,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  log.info('message', 'user message received', {sessionId: session.sessionId, contentLength: content.length});\n\n  const userEntry = createUserEntry(content);\n  const userIndex = appendEntry(session, userEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, userIndex, userEntry);\n\n  const parser = new ThinkingTagParser();\n  const state: StreamState = {\n    thinkingIndex: null,\n    assistantIndex: null,\n    suppressOutput: false,\n    sentAssistantIndices: [],\n  };\n\n  let textChunkCount = 0;\n\n  try {\n    for await (const chunk of orchestrator.processMessageStream(session, {signal})) {\n      // Its socket closed: breaking returns the run's generator, so the run stops and its own teardown still runs.\n      if (signal?.aborted) break;\n      textChunkCount = logChunk(chunk, textChunkCount);\n      processChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk);\n    }\n  } finally {\n    // Anything still open here was cut short: a stop has already flushed the parser and committed every entry it opened.\n    flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n  }\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n  log.info('stream', 'stream completed', {suppressed: state.suppressOutput, entries: session.entries.length});\n}\n\n// Diagnostics only: counts a run of text chunks so it is logged once, not per token.\nfunction logChunk(chunk: MessageChunk, textChunkCount: number): number {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return textChunkCount + 1;\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') {\n    log.debug('stream', 'tool_result chunk', {\n      messageCount: chunk.clientMessages.length,\n      types: chunk.clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n    });\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  log.debug('stream', 'chunk', {type: chunk.type});\n  return 0;\n}\n\nfunction processChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  chunk: MessageChunk\n): void {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return processTextChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk.text);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_use') return processToolUseChunk(ws, session, chunk.toolCall);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') return processToolResultChunk(ws, chunk.clientMessages);\n  if (chunk.type === 'suppress_output') return processSuppressChunk(ws, session, state);\n  if (chunk.type === 'stop') return processStopChunk(ws, session, state, parser);\n}\n\nfunction processTextChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  text: string\n): void {\n  const segments = parser.push(text);\n  for (const segment of segments) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction handleTextSegment(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, segment: ParsedSegment): void {\n  if (segment.type === 'thinking') {\n    streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex', createThinkingEntry, segment.content);\n    return;\n  }\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex', createAssistantEntry, segment.content);\n}\n\nfunction streamSegment(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  key: StateIndexKey,\n  createEntry: EntryFactory,\n  content: string\n): void {\n  const isNew = initSegment(session, state, key, createEntry);\n  if (isNew && state.suppressOutput) skipSuppressed(ws, session, state[key]!);\n  if (isNew && !state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, state[key]!, session.entries[state[key]!]);\n  appendDelta(session, state[key]!, content);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendDelta(ws, state[key]!, content);\n}\n\n// Stored as suppressed, so a replay on resume shows the client what it saw live and not the text a tool hid.\nfunction skipSuppressed(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  markSuppressed(session, index);\n  sendSkip(ws, index);\n}\n\nfunction markSuppressed(session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  const entry = session.entries[index];\n  if (entry.role === 'assistant' || entry.role === 'thinking') entry.suppressed = true;\n}\n\nfunction initSegment(session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey, createEntry: EntryFactory): boolean {\n  if (state[key] !== null) return false;\n  state[key] = appendEntry(session, createEntry());\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction commitAndClear(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n\n  commitEntry(session, index);\n  state[key] = null;\n\n  // Only what the client received can be retracted, and only assistant entries.\n  if (state.suppressOutput) return;\n\n  sendCommit(ws, index);\n  if (key === 'assistantIndex') state.sentAssistantIndices.push(index);\n}\n\nfunction processToolUseChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  toolCall: {name: string; input: Record<string, unknown>}\n): void {\n  const toolEntry = createToolEntry(toolCall.name, toolCall.input);\n  const toolIndex = appendEntry(session, toolEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, toolIndex, toolEntry);\n}\n\nfunction processToolResultChunk(ws: WebSocket, clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[]): void {\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    log.debug('stream', 'forwarding client message to frontend', {\n      type: msg.type,\n      index: 'index' in msg ? msg.index : undefined,\n    });\n    sendJson(ws, msg);\n  }\n}\n\n// Retracts what the client saw: every assistant entry sent so far is re-sent blank.\nfunction processSuppressChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  // Closed as streamed so far: an open answer is then retracted like a sent one, and what follows opens new entries.\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n  log.debug('stream', 'suppression activated', {blankingEntries: state.sentAssistantIndices});\n\n  for (const idx of state.sentAssistantIndices) {\n    sendUpsert(ws, idx, {...session.entries[idx], content: ''} as SessionEntry);\n    markSuppressed(session, idx);\n  }\n\n  state.sentAssistantIndices = [];\n  state.suppressOutput = true;\n}\n\nfunction processStopChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n}\n\n// The parser holds back a tail that might open a tag; without this the tail of a cut-off answer would be lost.\nfunction flushHeldText(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  for (const segment of parser.flush()) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\n// The flag lives on the stored entry even when suppressed; only a visible entry is re-sent, which is how a live client learns it.\nfunction closeCutOff(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n  markTruncated(session, index);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, index, session.entries[index]);\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, key);\n}\n\nfunction commitOpenEntries(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n}\n\nfunction handlePing(ws: WebSocket, timestamp: number): void {\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'pong', timestamp}));\n}\n\nasync function handleListSessions(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  sessionStore: SessionStore | undefined,\n  message: {type: 'list_sessions'; limit?: number; before?: {updatedAt: string; sessionId: string}}\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    if (!sessionStore?.listFor) {\n      sendSessionList(ws, []);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const summaries = await sessionStore.listFor(session.userId, {limit: message.limit, before: message.before});\n    const sessions = summaries.map(s => ({\n      sessionId: s.sessionId,\n      createdAt: s.createdAt.toISOString(),\n      updatedAt: s.updatedAt.toISOString(),\n      entryCount: s.entryCount,\n    }));\n    sendSessionList(ws, sessions);\n  } catch (error) {\n    const errorMsg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';\n    log.error('ws', 'listFor failed', {userId: session.userId, error: errorMsg});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Failed to list sessions');\n
